Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The hilbert variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 488 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 144 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by hilbert generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 144. QED.
